function SetWeaponType()
  ONEHANDED_SWORD = 11
  TWOHANDED_SWORD = 12
  ONEHANDED_AXE = 13
  TWOHANDED_AXE = 14
  ONEHANDED_BLUNT = 15
  TWOHANDED_BLUNT = 16
  BOW = 17
  CROSSBOW = 18
  STAFF = 19
  DAGGER = 20
  SHIELD = 21
  COM_BLUNT = 30
  COM_SWORD = 31
  OPP_HAMMER = 32
  OPP_AXE = 33
  OPP_SLUSHER = 34
  OPP_TALON = 35
  OPP_SYTHE = 36
end

function SetValue1(iSTR, iDEX, iCON, iINT, iWIS, iLevel, iRMinDmg, iRMaxDmg, iLMinDmg, iLMaxDmg, iDefense, iRHit, iLHit, iAvoid, iHPMax, iMPMax, iHPRec, iMPRec, iRCritical, iLCritical)
  g_iSTR = iSTR
  g_iDEX = iDEX
  g_iCON = iCON
  g_iINT = iINT
  g_iWIS = iWIS
  g_iLevel = iLevel
  g_iRMinDmg = iRMinDmg
  g_iRMaxDmg = iRMaxDmg
  g_iLMinDmg = iLMinDmg
  g_iLMaxDmg = iLMaxDmg
  g_iDefense = iDefense
  g_iRHit = iRHit
  g_iLHit = iLHit
  g_iAvoid = iAvoid
  g_iHPMax = iHPMax
  g_iMPMax = iMPMax
  g_iHPRec = iHPRec
  g_iMPRec = iMPRec
  g_iRCritical = iRCritical
  g_iLCritical = iLCritical
end

function SetValue2(iBlock, iSpeed, iCoolTime, iSkillPoint, iMagicPower, iMagicResist, fDefenseP, fCriticalMulti, fCriticalP, fBlockP, fSpeedP, fCooldownP, fMagicResistP, iSS, iSL)

  g_iCoolTime = iCoolTime
  g_iSkillPoint = iSkillPoint

  g_fSpeedP = fSpeedP
  g_fCooldownP = fCooldownP
  g_fMagicResistP = fMagicResistP
  g_iSS = iSS
  g_iSL = iSL
end

function SkillToolTipDesc_0x8101_1()
  local fSkillCooldown = 16 * g_fCooldownP / 100
  return "\\MP Consumption : 1" .. "\\Delay time : " .. fSkillCooldown .. " Second" .. "\\Range : 28 m" .. "\\Target : AOE" .. "\\Net effective time : 6 Second" .. "\\(Time will be shorter if opponent are higher level.)" .. "\\Not effective to monsters."
end

function SkillToolTipDesc_0x8102_1(Weapon)
  local fDmgAdd = (3 * g_iSL + g_iSS * 18) * (1 + g_ilevel * 0.02)
  local fHitAdd = 3 * g_iSL + g_iSS * 18
  return "\\Combo : 4 Hits" .. "\\(Increase as level increase.)" .. "\\0@Damage calculation: " .. fDmgAdd .. "\\0@HitRate Calculation : " .. fHitAdd
end

function MeleeSkill(MeleeDamage, a, b, c)
  local fSkillDamage = MeleeDamage * (a + b * (g_iSL + g_iSS * c))
  return fSkillDamage
end

function Probability(x)
end

function Chant(x)
  local fEnchantlevel = (g_iSL + (g_iSS + 1) * 6) * x * (100 + g_iMagicPower) / 100
  return fEnchantlevel
end

function MeleeEnchant(x)
  local fMeleeEnchant = ((g_iSS + 1) * 6 + g_iSL) * x
  return fMeleeEnchant
end

function InstanceMagicDamage(SkillDMG, RandomDamage)
  local fMATK = (g_iSL + (g_iSS + 1) * 6) * SkillDMG * (RandomDamage + g_iMagicPower) / 100
  return fMATK
end

function InstanceEnemyEnchant(x)
  local fEnemyEnchant = (g_iSL + (g_iSS + 1) * 6) * x
  return fEnemyEnchant
end

function InatanceMagicHeal(x, MinMaxHeal)
  local fHeal = (g_iSL + (g_iSS + 1) * 6) * x * (MinMaxHeal + g_iMagicPower) / 100
  return fHeal
end

function CastMagicHeal(x, MinMaxHeal)
  local fHeal = ((g_iSS + 1) * g_iSL + 1 + g_iSS * g_iSS * 5) * x * (MinMaxHeal + g_iMagicPower) / 100
  return fHeal
end

function HoldEnchant(x)
  local fHold = (g_iSS + g_iSL + g_iSS * 2) * x
  return fHold
end

function CastEnchant(x)
  local fEnchant = ((g_iSS + 1) * g_iSL + g_iSS * g_iSS) * x
  return fEnchant
end

function fUseMP(startMP, levelMP, lockADD)
  local lUseMP = startMP + lockADD + (levelMP + lockADD) * g_iSL
  return lUseMP
end

function InatanceMeeleHeal(x)
  local fHeal = (g_iSL + g_iSS * 6) * x * (100 + g_iSS) / 100
  return fHeal
end

function GetDemage(a, b, c)
  return g_iRMinDmg * (a + b * (g_iSL + g_iSS * c))
end

function SkillToolTipDesc_0x8104_1(Weapon)
  local fMinDmg = MeleeSkill(g_iRMinDmg, 1.5, 0.1, 6)
  local fMaxDmg = MeleeSkill(g_iRMaxDmg, 1.5, 0.1, 6)
  local fSkillUseMP = fUseMP(0, 40, 0)
  local fEnchantlevel = g_iSS * 6 + g_iSL
  local fEnchantTime = ((g_iSS + 1) * 6 + g_iSL) * 0.5
  local fSkillCooldown = 12 * g_fCooldownP / 100
  if Weapon == ONEHANDED_SWORD then
    local fMinDmg = MeleeSkill(g_iRMinDmg, 1.5, 0.15, 6)
    local fMaxDmg = MeleeSkill(g_iRMaxDmg, 1.5, 0.15, 6)
  elseif Weapon == TWOHANDED_SWORD then
    local fMinDmg = MeleeSkill(g_iRMinDmg, 1.5, 0.15, 6)
    local fMaxDmg = MeleeSkill(g_iRMaxDmg, 1.5, 0.15, 6)
  elseif Weapon == ONEHANDED_AXE then
    local fEnchantlevel = g_iSS * 6 + g_iSL
  elseif Weapon == TWOHANDED_AXE then
    local fEnchantlevel = g_iSS * 6 + g_iSL
  elseif Weapon == ONEHANDED_BLUNT then
    local fEnchantTime = ((g_iSS + 1) * 6 + g_iSL) * 0.5
  elseif Weapon == TWOHANDED_BLUNT then
    local fEnchantTime = ((g_iSS + 1) * 6 + g_iSL) * 0.5
  end
  return "\\0@MP Consumption : " .. fSkillUseMP .. "\\0@Damage : " .. fMinDmg .. "~" .. fMaxDmg .. "\\0@Delay time : " .. fSkillCooldown .. "Second" .. "\\Attack Range : Close range" .. "\\0@Armor reduced time : 20 Second" .. "\\0@Armor reduced level : " .. fEnchantlevel .. "\\0@Confuse : " .. fEnchantTime .. " Second" .. "\\0@Slow motion effect : " .. fEnchantTime .. " Second"
end

  local fMaxDmg = MeleeSkill(g_iRMaxDmg, 1.5, 0.1, 6)
  local fSkillUseMP = fUseMP(0, 40, 0)
  local fEnchantlevel = g_iSS * 6 + g_iSL
  local fEnchantTime = ((g_iSS + 1) * 6 + g_iSL) * 0.5
  local fSkillCooldown = 12 * g_fCooldownP / 100
  if Weapon == COM_SWORD then
    local fMinDmg = MeleeSkill(g_iRMinDmg, 1.5, 0.15, 6)
    local fMaxDmg = MeleeSkill(g_iRMaxDmg, 1.5, 0.15, 6)
  elseif Weapon == COM_BLUNT then
    local fEnchantlevel = g_iSS * 6 + g_iSL
  elseif Weapon == OPP_AXE then
    local fEnchantTime = ((g_iSS + 1) * 6 + g_iSL) * 0.5
  elseif Weapon == OPP_HAMMER then
    local fEnchantTime = ((g_iSS + 1) * 6 + g_iSL) * 0.5
  end
  return "\\0@MP Consumption : " .. fSkillUseMP .. "\\0@Damage : " .. fMinDmg .. "~" .. fMaxDmg .. "\\0@Delay time : " .. fSkillCooldown .. "Second" .. "\\Attack Range : Close range" .. "\\0@Armor reduced time : 20 Second" .. "\\0@Armor reduced level : " .. fEnchantlevel .. "\\0@Confuse : " .. fEnchantTime .. " Second" .. "\\0@Slow motion effect : " .. fEnchantTime .. " Second"
end
